_____________________ TestDataDelete.test_clear_all_memory _____________________

self = <tests.test_safety_prod.TestDataDelete object at 0x10cd51940>
client = <starlette.testclient.TestClient object at 0x1207d1a30>

    def test_clear_all_memory(self, client):
        """用户可清空所有记忆"""
        # 先创建记忆
        client.post(
            "/api/v1/settings/memory",
            params={
                "user_id": "delete-test-user",
                "type": "preference",
                "content": "待清空记忆",
            }
        )
        # 清空
